===== CORE_DOCTRINE BEGIN =====
LABEL: Core Doctrine

# PEARL_0000 - The Tinkularity

Status: CORE DOCTRINE SEED
Owner: Speaker / TinkerDen
Source Packet: BIRD_0034_MAKER_CONTEXT_BOOTLOADER

## Core Doctrine

The organism must load its own memory before it acts.

Ben is the Operator, not the transport layer, not the clipboard, not the context mule, and not the manual bootloader. If an Aeye needs the Book, the frontier, the current repo reality, or the shared state, the machine must fetch it from the repo.

TinkerDen is a command center, not a chat surface. Mission Control explains why. Bridge shows now. Engine Room shows execution. Speaker preserves causal memory. Wormeyes reports physical reality. The Corpus Callosum keeps Aeyes aware of shared frontier state.

Escape velocity means the system can end a session and wake back up with its doctrine, frontier, shared state, and local reality physically reloaded from disk.

## Non-Negotiables

- Do not summarize the Book when raw source is required.
- Do not make Ben upload the Book again.
- Do not treat a sender-side write as delivery proof.
- Do not overwrite Speaker memory without source notes.
- Do not route around human gates.
- Do not let governance live only in an LLM prompt when it can live in a file, hook, script, database, or receipt.

## Operating Lesson

Memory is infrastructure. If memory depends on Ben manually pasting context, the organism is still a puppet. If memory is compiled from canonical files at boot, the organism can start becoming a system.

===== CORE_DOCTRINE END =====

# Organism Frontier

Status: CURRENT RULES SEED
Owner: TinkerDen / Nervous System
Source Packet: BIRD_0034_MAKER_CONTEXT_BOOTLOADER

## Current Rule

HALT PERIPHERAL BUILD until the nervous system can load memory on boot.

The active frontier is to make the Aeyes ingest the Book, the current doctrine, shared frontier state, and Wormeyes reality without Ben uploading context manually.

## Current Construction Lane

- Build `tinkarden/nervous_system/bootloader.js`.
- Compile raw context into `tinkarden/nervous_system/active_context.txt`.
- Keep `shared_frontier.json` as the Corpus Callosum state.
- Keep `world_state.json` as the Wormeyes physical reality sensor.
- Let `aeye_client.js` call the bootloader before provider calls.

## Boundary

This file is raw source for the bootloader. The bootloader may concatenate it, label it, and report byte counts. The bootloader must not summarize it.
